# RIBDIGI ERP — Production Readiness Gate

RIBDIGI is intended to be a commercial ERP, not a demo application. A feature is **production-ready only when implementation, automated tests, security controls, and operational requirements are verified**.

## Non-negotiable release rules

- No demo tenant, sample company, fake transaction, pre-filled login, or known default password in production.
- No endpoint may return success for work that was not actually completed.
- No hard-coded secrets or development credentials in production.
- No protected tenant data endpoint without authentication, tenant isolation and authorization.
- No database schema change outside versioned migrations.
- No financial workflow is complete until stock, ledger, tax, credit and audit side effects are transactionally correct where applicable.
- No production claim until the Phase 5 launch gates in `docs/DEVELOPMENT_ROADMAP.md` are passed.

## Required launch gates

### Platform & tenancy
- [x] Schema-per-tenant strategy implemented as specified, or architecture documents formally revised to an approved equivalent.
  - Approved equivalent: shared-schema + `tenant_id` (see `docs/ADR_001_TENANCY.md`). Schema-per-tenant remains post-MVP.
- [x] Cross-tenant isolation integration tests pass for every tenant-owned resource.
  - Complete (shared-schema MVP): SQLite suite covers product list scoping, foreign sales invoice 404, mismatched `X-Tenant-ID` (products, credit aging, users), suspend/activate login block, plus matrix coverage for customers/suppliers, credit statement/limit, product image, expenses (+ attachment + OCR-suggest + OCR-apply + foreign `store_id`/`liquid_account_id` 404), purchase invoices (+ attachment + OCR-suggest + OCR-apply), tax rates, store inventory, POS sessions/drawer/receipt (+ receipt-send), notifications, backups (+ download), accounting accounts/journals, audit logs, bank connections (+ create with foreign `account_id` 404), bank-statement create/import foreign `account_id` 404, stock-in foreign `warehouse_id` 404 (tenant-scoped warehouse guard on stock engine); Stage 18 S1: API keys/webhooks/OCR-apply/stock-counts/warehouse-transfers/quotations/orders/product warehouse-stock + mismatched header on keys/webhooks (`test_isolation_matrix_s1.py`), customer/supplier payment foreign `liquid_account_id` 404 (eager settlement GL resolve), store create foreign `manager_id` 404, report schedules, cheques, stock transfers, PO print, product labels, media-key tenant mismatch (unit + planted wrong key on owned expense/logo HTTP 403), tenant-scoped AI insights (chat remains 503 until provider), and Stage 1 B6 paths: users CSV import (header mismatch + tenant-scoped commit + foreign branch_code), `GET/PATCH /settings/email` (header mismatch + no cross-tenant SMTP mutate), warehouse PATCH (foreign id/manager + header mismatch). Stage 21 I1: BR-1.4–1.5 isolation + seed fidelity (`test_tenant_isolation_seeds_i1.py`). Stage 21 D1: `docs/STAGE_21_FIDELITY.md` (`test_stage21_fidelity_d1.py`). Stage 23 I1: finance/report residual matrix — liquid accounts/transfers, expense categories/recurring, foreign branch + BS/P&L/cash-flow `store_id`/`branch_id`, mismatched header on financial reports/liquid/budgets (`test_isolation_matrix_i1.py`). Stage 23 G1: commercial MVP gate closure marks shared-schema isolation Complete (`test_mvp_gate_closure_g1.py`). Remaining post-MVP: schema-per-tenant (ADR-001).
- [x] Tenant provisioning, suspension, activation and lifecycle management complete.
  - Complete (MVP): register + defaults, company profile GET/PATCH, suspend (revokes sessions) / activate, super_admin cross-tenant lifecycle, trial `trial_ends_at` (default 14d) + 7/3/1 reminders + grace read-only (`status=grace`) then auto-suspend, company logo upload/serve/delete via `STORAGE_BACKEND=local|s3` (MinIO/S3-compatible object store; keys remain tenant-scoped). Invoice/receipt/quotation/credit-note prints embed logo (HTML data-URI) and prefer `legal_name` on documents (BR-20.1). Document numbering prefixes/series; invoice print template (`a4`/`thermal_*`); POS receipt print template (`thermal_80`/`thermal_58` default paper); optional `document_header`/`document_footer` on prints (BR-20.4). `plan_code` is commercial metadata only with `billing_deferred` (ADR-002); no fake payment success on upgrade. Stage 21 closed (ADR-048): Tenant Lifecycle, Org & Dashboard Fidelity — `docs/STAGE_21_PLAN.md`, exit `docs/STAGE_21_EXIT_CRITERIA.md`. Stage 21 T1: BR-1.1–1.3 lifecycle fidelity (`test_tenant_lifecycle_t1.py`). Stage 21 I1: BR-1.4–1.5 isolation + seeds (`test_tenant_isolation_seeds_i1.py`). Stage 21 O1–N1: org/users/dashboard/notifications fidelity proofs. Stage 21 D1/H21x: BR-1–4 / readiness / USER_MANUAL / API / launch §§1–2 sync + freeze (`docs/STAGE_21_FIDELITY.md`, `test_stage21_fidelity_d1.py`, `test_stage21_exit_h21x.py`, ADR-048). ### Identity & security
- [x] Login/logout/refresh token flow complete.
  - Stage 19 K1: live JWT login/refresh rotation + API-key regression + tenant-scoped rate-limit headers (`test_auth_api_fidelity_k1.py`; BR-18.1).
  - Stage 19 P1: products/catalog CRUD + import + stock/barcode lookup and customers/groups/balance/history via JWT + X-API-Key reads (`test_products_customers_api_p1.py`; BR-18.2–18.3).
  - Stage 19 S1: sales quote/order/invoice/payment/return/POS and purchasing PR/PO/GRN/PI/supplier payment via JWT + X-API-Key reads (`test_sales_purchases_api_s1.py`; BR-18.4–18.5).
  - Stage 19 A1: API standards — `env()` envelope, `/api/v1`, list `limit`, OpenAPI (non-prod), webhooks (`test_api_standards_a1.py`; BR-18.6).
  - Stage 19 U1: BR-19 auth/2FA/sessions fidelity (`test_auth_session_br19_u1.py`).
  - Stage 19 C1: BR-20 company/settings fidelity — legal/logo/formats/SMTP/numbering/print templates (`test_company_settings_br20_c1.py`; WYSIWYG designer deferred).
  - Stage 19 R1: LAUNCH §5 reliability — Redis cache soft-fail, permissions invalidation, Celery beat matrix, admin jobs dry-run, logical DR runbook (`test_reliability_cache_r1.py`; `docs/DR_LOGICAL_BACKUP_RUNBOOK.md`).
  - Stage 19 D1: BR-18–20 / SECURITY_GUIDE / API docs / readiness / launch §§1–5 fidelity (`docs/STAGE_19_FIDELITY.md`, `test_stage19_fidelity_d1.py`).
  - Stage 19 H19x: exit met — `docs/STAGE_19_EXIT_CRITERIA.md`, freeze `docs/ADR_044_STAGE19_FREEZE.md` (`test_stage19_exit_h19x.py`).
- [x] Email verification and password reset complete.
  - Tokens issued; SMTP delivery with console fallback in dev; production requires `SMTP_HOST` + `SMTP_FROM_EMAIL` when `EMAIL_ENABLED=true`; tenants may override SMTP via encrypted `PATCH /settings/email`. Login blocked until email verified (`EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED` + `POST /auth/resend-verification`). Authenticated `POST /auth/change-password` revokes other sessions.
- [x] 2FA/TOTP complete for required roles.
  - Complete: TOTP setup/QR/confirm, backup codes, encrypted secrets, company_admin/super_admin enrollment gate; WebAuthn/passkeys (register/list/delete + login challenge with `methods`); MFA satisfied by TOTP and/or passkeys.
- [x] Session listing and revocation complete.
  - Complete: list/revoke sessions; Shell idle timer calls `POST /auth/idle-logout` to revoke the current session JWT server-side (audit `idle_logout`) before clearing client tokens (BR-19.3 inactivity auto-logout).
